| Beşkardeşler Kaya Mezarları (`ee94fe5e-d975-4ed9-940d-16056532293e`) | Türkiye | - | empty | Only the Kırıkhan municipality attributes these rock tombs to a period, and it does so in Turkish ('Helenistik dönemden kalma kaya mezarları', 'Helenistik dönemden izler taşıyan bu yapı'). No source that states the period of this site in a verifiable form was found, so the field is left empty rather than filled from a translation of the municipality's wording; the Hellenistic attribution is noted here for the owner. |
| Mahkeme Ağacin Kültürel Jeositi (`1ac10af9-1ace-4be6-8e02-88dacc77b6c6`) | Türkiye | - | empty | The municipality only says the first inhabitants were probably the earliest Christians hiding from the wrath of Rome and that the caves were turned into homes and churches; it names no period, year or century for the start of this site, and no other source read does. |
